今日最新實盤掃描（10/30 結算）：大盤暴跌 667 點下僅 2 檔 逆勢創歷史高點

查核基準日 2026/10/29（週四）：台股盤中狂瀉逾 980 點、終場跌 667 點。前一日（10/28）入選的 3 檔中，6199 天品 逆勢收紅 158.50 元（+1.60%，日 K 連 9 紅）續創掛牌歷史新天價；而上市週轉率冠軍 4526 東台 爆出 10.9 萬張巨量、週轉率高達 42.78% 逆勢大漲 +6.34% 收 52.80 元（正式追平 2011 年未還原歷史最高價 52.80 元、還原權值續創歷史新高）。至於 10/28 入選的 3498 陽程（10/29 收 256.0 元，-2.85%）與 1569 濱川（10/29 收 92.3 元，-6.77%）因隨大盤回檔未於 10/29 創高，已自動移入「歷史選股每日監控日誌」持續追蹤漲跌！

AI 伺服器高階 PCB 鑽孔機長單排至 2027 年底＋歐洲工具機通路併購效益

📌 10/29 查核：10/28 漲停收 49.65 元時距 2011 年未還原最高價 52.80 元尚差 3.15 元；10/29 逆勢大漲 +6.34% 收在 52.80 元，正式追平未還原歷史最高收盤價，且還原權值股價大幅刷新歷史天價！

高階 PCB 鑽孔機訂單受惠 AI 伺服器高層數板與 ABF 載板擴產，長單能見度直達 2027 年底，並切入碳化矽（SiC）晶圓減薄與先進封裝雷射加工設備。2026 年 9 月營收 6.53 億元（月增 45.46%、年增 81.03%）。10/29 在大盤重挫 667 點下爆出 109,029 張天量（較前日再增 34,847 張）、週轉率高達 42.78%，收盤 52.80 元正式追平 2011 年未還原歷史最高價！

2026/10/29 實盤關鍵數據明細：
✔ 10/29 逆勢大漲 +3.15 元（+6.34%）收 52.80 元，近六個營業日累積收盤漲幅達 41.19%（證交所注意股公告）。
✔ 10/29 成交量達 109,029 張（較 10/28 之 74,182 張再增 34,847 張，為 60 日均量之 14.89 倍），週轉率 42.78% 冠絕全市場。
✔ 9 月營收年增 +81.03%，PCB 鑽孔機與半導體設備雙引擎帶動營運爆發。

子公司奈科潔淨科技獨門 R01 配方切入 AI 伺服器水冷板與先進封裝清洗

📌 10/29 查核：10/28 收 156.0 元創高後，10/29 在大盤重挫 667 點下逆勢收紅 158.50 元（+1.60%），日 K 連 9 漲、嚴格續創掛牌以來歷史收盤新天價！

旗下持股 70% 之「奈科潔淨科技」憑藉獨家研發「R01」高階潔淨配方、Class 10/100 無塵室與 ISO 16232 檢測能量，切入全球雲端巨頭與 AI 伺服器液冷／水冷散熱系統（水冷板 Cold Plate、分歧管）及先進封裝元件高潔淨清洗。2026 年 9 月營收年增高達 146% 創單月歷史新高；10/29 獲外資逆勢買超 372 張，展現極強籌碼韌性。

</script><script data-bard-client-injected="true">(function() {
  const SCROLL_KEYS = new Set([
    'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight',
    ' ', 'Spacebar', 'PageUp', 'PageDown', 'Home', 'End',
  ]);
  const HORIZONTAL_KEYS = new Set(['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight']);

  function isTextEntryTarget(target) {
    if (!target) return false;
    const tagName = target.tagName;
    return tagName === 'INPUT' || tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
        tagName === 'SELECT' || target.isContentEditable === true;
  }

  // An element absorbs the key only if it both overflows on the axis being
  // scrolled and is configured to scroll on that axis. The cheap dimension
  // test runs first because getComputedStyle forces a style recalc, and this
  // runs on every keypress. Each axis is read from its own longhand: the
  // 'overflow' shorthand serializes as two values when the axes differ, which
  // is the common 'overflow-x: hidden; overflow-y: auto' pane.
  function canScroll(node, horizontal) {
    const overflows = horizontal ? node.scrollWidth > node.clientWidth
                                 : node.scrollHeight > node.clientHeight;
    if (!overflows) return false;
    const style = window.getComputedStyle(node);
    const overflow = horizontal ? style.overflowX : style.overflowY;
    return overflow === 'auto' || overflow === 'scroll';
  }

  // Walks up from the event target so nested scrollers keep their behavior.
  // <body> is included because pages that pin the root height make it the
  // scroll container.
  function hasScrollableAncestor(target, horizontal) {
    let node = target;
    while (node && node !== document.documentElement) {
      if (canScroll(node, horizontal)) return true;
      node = node.parentElement;
    }
    return false;
  }

  function rootCanScroll(horizontal) {
    const scroller = document.scrollingElement || document.documentElement;
    if (!scroller) return false;
    // Tolerate a pixel of rounding so subpixel layouts do not look scrollable.
    return horizontal ? scroller.scrollWidth > window.innerWidth + 1
                      : scroller.scrollHeight > window.innerHeight + 1;
  }

  window.addEventListener('keydown', function(event) {
    if (!SCROLL_KEYS.has(event.key)) return;
    if (event.defaultPrevented) return;
    // Alt and Meta turn arrows into browser history navigation, and Ctrl is
    // reserved for shortcuts, so none of them are scroll intent.
    if (event.altKey || event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey) return;

    const target = event.target;
    if (isTextEntryTarget(target)) return;

    // Anything that can still absorb the scroll gets to keep it.
    const horizontal = HORIZONTAL_KEYS.has(event.key);
    if (rootCanScroll(horizontal)) return;
    if (hasScrollableAncestor(target, horizontal)) return;

    event.preventDefault();
  }, {capture: true, passive: false});
})();</script><script data-bard-client-injected="true">(function() {
  // Ensure this script is executed only once

    return;

  let nextTokenPromiseId = 0;

  // Stores { resolve, reject } for ongoing token requests
  const pendingTokenPromises = {};

  // Listen for messages from the Host Application
  window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {

    const messageData = event.data;

  if (messageData && messageData.type === 'RESOLVE_NEW_FIREBASE_TOKEN') {
      const { success, token, error, promiseId } = messageData ?? {};
      if (pendingTokenPromises[promiseId]) {
        if (success) {
          pendingTokenPromises[promiseId].resolve(token);
        } else {
          pendingTokenPromises[promiseId].reject(new Error(error || 'Token refresh failed from host.'));
        }
        delete pendingTokenPromises[promiseId];
      }
    }
  });

  // Expose a function for the Generated App to request a new Firebase token
  window.requestNewFirebaseToken = function() {
    const currentPromiseId = nextTokenPromiseId++;
    const promise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      pendingTokenPromises[currentPromiseId] = { resolve, reject };
    });
    if (window.parent && window.parent !== window) {
      window.parent.postMessage({
        type: 'REQUEST_NEW_FIREBASE_TOKEN',
        promiseId: currentPromiseId
      }, '*');
    } else {
      pendingTokenPromises[currentPromiseId].reject(new Error('No parent window to request token from.'));
      delete pendingTokenPromises[currentPromiseId];
    }
    return promise;
  };

(function() {
  const pendingMediaResolvers = {};
  let nextMediaPromiseId = 0;

  function requestMediaPermissions(constraints) {
    const mediaPromiseId = nextMediaPromiseId++;
    const promise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      pendingMediaResolvers[mediaPromiseId] = (granted) => {
        delete pendingMediaResolvers[mediaPromiseId];
        resolve(granted);
      };
    });

    window.parent.postMessage({
      type: 'requestMediaPermission',
      constraints: constraints,
      promiseId: mediaPromiseId,
    }, '*');

    return promise;
  }

  let originalGetUserMedia = realOriginalGetUserMedia;

  function interceptGetUserMedia() {
    if (navigator.mediaDevices) {
      Object.defineProperty(navigator.mediaDevices, 'getUserMedia', {
        value: function(constraints) {
          return requestMediaPermissions(constraints).then((granted) => {
            if (granted) {
              if (originalGetUserMedia) {
                return originalGetUserMedia(constraints);
              } else {
                throw new Error("Original getUserMedia not available.");
              }
            } else {
              throw new DOMException('Permission denied', 'NotAllowedError');
            }
          });
        },
        writable: false,
        configurable: false
      });
    }
  }

  interceptGetUserMedia();

  const observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutationsList, observer) {
    for (const mutation of mutationsList) {
      if (mutation.type === 'reconfigured' && mutation.name === 'getUserMedia' && mutation.object === navigator.mediaDevices) {
        interceptGetUserMedia();
      } else if (mutation.type === 'attributes' && mutation.attributeName === 'getUserMedia' && mutation.target === navigator.mediaDevices) {
        interceptGetUserMedia();
      } else if (mutation.type === 'childList' && mutation.addedNodes) {
        mutation.addedNodes.forEach(node => {
          if (node === navigator.mediaDevices) {
            interceptGetUserMedia();
          }
        });
      }
    }
  });

  function interceptSpeechRecognition() {
    if (!window.SpeechRecognition && !window.webkitSpeechRecognition) {
      return;
    }

    const OriginalSpeechRecognition = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;

    const SpeechRecognitionWrapper = function(...args) {
      const recognizer = new OriginalSpeechRecognition(...args);
      const originalStart = recognizer.start.bind(recognizer);

      recognizer.start = function() {
        requestMediaPermissions({ audio: true }).then(granted => {
          if (granted) {
            originalStart();
          } else {
            const errorEvent = new SpeechRecognitionErrorEvent('error');
            errorEvent.error = 'not-allowed'; // This is the standard error for permission denial.
            recognizer.dispatchEvent(errorEvent);
          }
        });
      };

      return recognizer;
    };

    SpeechRecognitionWrapper.prototype = OriginalSpeechRecognition.prototype;
    SpeechRecognitionWrapper.prototype.constructor = SpeechRecognitionWrapper;

    if (window.SpeechRecognition) {
      window.SpeechRecognition = SpeechRecognitionWrapper;
    }
    if (window.webkitSpeechRecognition) {
      window.webkitSpeechRecognition = SpeechRecognitionWrapper;
    }
  }

  interceptSpeechRecognition();

  window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
    if (event.data) {
      if (event.data.type === 'resolveMediaPermission') {
        const { promiseId, granted } = event.data;
        if (pendingMediaResolvers[promiseId]) {
          pendingMediaResolvers[promiseId](granted);
        }
      }
    }
  });

  const pendingFetchResolvers = {};

  function handleStringInput(input, optionsArgument) {
    const actualUrl = input;
    const fetchCallArgs = [actualUrl, optionsArgument];
    const effectiveOptions = optionsArgument || {};
    const bodyForApiKeyCheck = effectiveOptions.body;
    const bodyForPostMessage = effectiveOptions.body;
    return { actualUrl, fetchCallArgs, effectiveOptions, bodyForApiKeyCheck, bodyForPostMessage };
  }

  function handleRequestInput(input, optionsArgument) {
    const actualUrl = input.url;
    const fetchCallArgs = [input, optionsArgument];
    const effectiveOptions = { method: input.method, headers: new Headers(input.headers) };
    let bodyForApiKeyCheck;
    let bodyForPostMessage;

    if (optionsArgument) {
      if (optionsArgument.method) effectiveOptions.method = optionsArgument.method;
      if (optionsArgument.headers) effectiveOptions.headers = new Headers(optionsArgument.headers);
      if ('body' in optionsArgument) {
        bodyForApiKeyCheck = optionsArgument.body;
        bodyForPostMessage = optionsArgument.body;
      } else {
        bodyForApiKeyCheck = undefined;
        bodyForPostMessage = input.body;
      }
    } else {
      bodyForApiKeyCheck = undefined;
      bodyForPostMessage = input.body;
    }
    return { actualUrl, fetchCallArgs, effectiveOptions, bodyForApiKeyCheck, bodyForPostMessage };
  }

  window.fetch = function(input, optionsArgument) {
    let actualUrl;
    let fetchCallArgs;

    let bodyForApiKeyCheck;
    let bodyForPostMessage;

    if (typeof input === 'string') {
      ({actualUrl, fetchCallArgs, effectiveOptions, bodyForApiKeyCheck, bodyForPostMessage} = handleStringInput(input, optionsArgument));

      if(apiKeyIsNull) {
        const promiseId = nextPromiseId++;
        const promise = new Promise((resolve) => {
          pendingFetchResolvers[promiseId] = (resolvedResponse) => {
            delete pendingFetchResolvers[promiseId];
            resolve(resolvedResponse);
          };
        });

        let serializedBodyForPostMessage;
        if (typeof bodyForPostMessage === 'string' || bodyForPostMessage == null) {
            serializedBodyForPostMessage = bodyForPostMessage;
        } else if (bodyForPostMessage instanceof ReadableStream) {
            serializedBodyForPostMessage = null;
        } else {
            try {
                serializedBodyForPostMessage = JSON.stringify(bodyForPostMessage);
            } catch (e) {
                serializedBodyForPostMessage = null;
            }
        }

        const messageOptions = {
            method: effectiveOptions.method,
            headers: Object.fromEntries(new Headers(effectiveOptions.headers).entries()),
            body: serializedBodyForPostMessage
        };

        window.parent.postMessage({
          type: 'requestFetch',
          url: actualUrl,
          modelName: modelName,
          options: messageOptions,
          promiseId: promiseId,
        }, '*');

        return promise;
      }
      return originalFetch.apply(window, fetchCallArgs);
    }
    return originalFetch.apply(window, fetchCallArgs);
  };

  window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
    if (event.data && event.data.type === 'resolveFetch') {
      const { promiseId, response } = event.data;
      if (pendingFetchResolvers[promiseId]) {
        try {
          const reconstructedResponse = new Response(response.body, {
            status: response.status,
            statusText: response.statusText,
            headers: new Headers(response.headers),
          });
          pendingFetchResolvers[promiseId](reconstructedResponse);
        } catch (error) {
          pendingFetchResolvers[promiseId](new Response(null, { status: 500, statusText: "Interceptor Response Reconstruction Error" }));
        }
      }
    }
  });

  function normalizeSlug(str) {
    try {
      return str
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^p{L}p{N}s_-]/gu, '')
        .replace(/s+/g, '-');
    } catch (e) {
      return str
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9s_-]/g, '')
        .replace(/s+/g, '-');
    }
  }

  function findTargetElement(hash) {
    if (!hash || hash === '#') {
      return null;
    }
    const rawId = hash.startsWith('#') ? hash.slice(1) : hash;
    let decodedId = rawId;
    try {
      decodedId = decodeURIComponent(rawId);
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore URI malformed errors.
    }

    // 1. Direct ID match (raw and decoded).
    let target = document.getElementById(rawId) || document.getElementById(decodedId);
    if (target) {
      return target;
    }

    // 2. Name attribute match (<a name="...">).
    try {
      if (typeof CSS !== 'undefined' && CSS.escape) {
        target =
          document.querySelector('[name="' + CSS.escape(rawId) + '"]') ||
          document.querySelector('[name="' + CSS.escape(decodedId) + '"]');
      } else {
        const namedElements = document.getElementsByName(rawId);
        if (namedElements && namedElements.length > 0) {
          return namedElements[0];
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore querySelector syntax errors.
    }
    if (target) {
      return target;
    }

    // 3. Match heading text by slug (for Markdown TOCs where headings lack explicit IDs).
    const targetSlug = normalizeSlug(decodedId);
    if (targetSlug) {
      for (const heading of document.querySelectorAll('h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6')) {
        if (normalizeSlug(heading.textContent || '') === targetSlug) {
          return heading;
        }
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function(event) {
    const target = event.target;
    const anchor = target && typeof target.closest === 'function' ? target.closest('a') : null;
    if (!anchor) {
      return;
    }

    const href = anchor.getAttribute('href');
    if (
      !href ||
      href.trim().toLowerCase().startsWith('javascript:') ||
      anchor.hasAttribute('download')
    ) {
      return;
    }

    // Check if the link is an in-page hash / anchor link.
    if (href.startsWith('#')) {
      event.preventDefault();
      const targetElement = findTargetElement(href);
      if (targetElement) {
        targetElement.scrollIntoView({behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start'});
      }
      return;
    }

    // Check if the URL has the same origin/pathname but with a hash.
    try {
      const url = new URL(anchor.href, window.location.href);
      if (
        url.hash &&
        url.origin === window.location.origin &&
        url.pathname === window.location.pathname
      ) {
        event.preventDefault();
        const targetElement = findTargetElement(url.hash);
        if (targetElement) {
          targetElement.scrollIntoView({behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start'});
        }
        return;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Fall through if URL parsing fails.
    }

    // For all external or full navigation links, prevent iframe-level navigation
    // (which breaks in AGSA WebView or sandboxed iframes) and ensure they open
    // safely in a new tab/window.
    if (anchor.target !== '_blank') {
      event.preventDefault();
      window.open(anchor.href, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
    }
  }, {capture: true});

</script><script data-bard-client-injected="true">(function() {
  const originalConsoleLog = console.log;
  const originalConsoleError = console.error;

    /**
   * Normalizes an error event or a promise rejection reason into a structured error object.
   * @param {*} errorEventOrReason The error object or reason.
   * @return {object} Structured error data { message, name, stack }.
   */
  function getErrorObject(errorEventOrReason) {
    if (errorEventOrReason instanceof Error) {
      return {
        message: errorEventOrReason.message,
        name: errorEventOrReason.name,
        stack: errorEventOrReason.stack,
      };
    }
    // Fallback for non-Error objects.
    try {
      return {
        message: JSON.stringify(errorEventOrReason),
        name: 'UnknownErrorType',
        stack: null,
      };
    } catch (e) {
      return {
        message: String(errorEventOrReason),
        name: 'UnknownErrorTypeNonStringifiable',
        stack: null,
      };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Converts an array of arguments (from log/error) into a single string.
   * Handles Error objects specially to include their message and stack.
   * @param {Array<*>} args - Arguments passed to console methods.
   * @return {string} A string representation of the arguments.
   */
  function stringifyArgs(args) {
    return args
      .map((arg) => {
        if (arg instanceof Error) {
          const {message, stack} = arg;
          return `Error: ${message}${stack ? ('\nStack: ' + stack) : ''}`;
        }
        if (typeof arg === 'object' && arg !== null) {
          try {
            return JSON.stringify(arg);
          } catch (error) {
            return '[Circular Object]';
          }
        } else {
          return String(arg);
        }
      })
      .join(' ');
  }

  console.log = function(...args) {
    const logString = stringifyArgs(args);
    window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'log', message: logString }, '*');
    originalConsoleLog.apply(console, args);
  };

  console.error = function(...args) {
    let errorData;
    if (args.length > 0 && args[0] instanceof Error) {
      const err = args[0];
      // If the first arg is an Error, capture its details.
      errorData = {
        type: 'error',
        source: 'CONSOLE_ERROR',
        ...getErrorObject(err),
        rawArgsString: stringifyArgs(args.slice(1)),
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
    } else {
      // If not an Error object, treat all args as a general error message.
      errorData = {
        type: 'error',
        source: 'CONSOLE_ERROR',
        message: stringifyArgs(args),
        name: 'ConsoleLoggedError',
        stack: null,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
    }
    window.parent.postMessage(errorData, '*');
    originalConsoleError.apply(console, args);
  };

  // Listen for global unhandled synchronous errors.
  window.addEventListener('error', function(event) {
    const errorDetails = event.error ? getErrorObject(event.error) : {
      message: event.message,
      name: 'GlobalError',
      stack: null,
      filename: event.filename,
      lineno: event.lineno,
      colno: event.colno,
    };

    window.parent.postMessage({
      type: 'error',
      source: 'global',
      ...errorDetails,
      message: errorDetails.message || event.message,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    }, '*');
  });

  // Listen for unhandled promise rejections (asynchronous errors).
  window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', function(event) {
    const errorDetails = getErrorObject(event.reason);

    window.parent.postMessage({
      type: 'error',
      source: 'unhandledrejection',
      ...errorDetails,
      message: errorDetails.message || 'Unhandled Promise Rejection',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    }, '*');
  });

})();</script>

    function getHistoryLogs() {
      try {
        const stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY_HISTORY);
        if (stored) return JSON.parse(stored);
      } catch (e) {}
      return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_HISTORY_LOGS));
    }

    function saveHistoryLogs(logs) {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_HISTORY, JSON.stringify(logs));
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    function renderAuditTable() {
      const tbody = document.getElementById('auditTableBody');
      if (!tbody) return;
      tbody.innerHTML = '';
      AUDIT_CANDIDATES.forEach(item => {
        const isPass = item.c1 === true && item.c2 === true && item.c3 === true;
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.className = isPass ? 'bg-rose-950/25 hover:bg-rose-950/40 transition' : 'hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition';
        tr.innerHTML = `
          <td class="py-3 px-3 font-mono font-bold ${isPass ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-slate-300'}">${item.rank}</td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 font-mono font-bold text-white">${item.id} ${item.name}</td>
          <td class="py-3 px-2"><span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] bg-slate-800 text-cyan-400 border border-slate-700">${item.market}</span></td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 text-right font-mono ${item.close.includes('▼') ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'} font-bold">${item.close}</td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 text-right font-mono text-slate-400">${item.prev}</td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 text-right font-mono text-amber-400 font-bold">${item.turnover}</td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center">${item.c1 ? '<span class="text-emerald-400 font-bold">✔ 合格</span>' : '<span class="text-rose-400 font-bold">✘ 未進前30</span>'}</td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center">${item.c2 === true ? '<span class="text-emerald-400 font-bold">✔ 10/29創高</span>' : '<span class="text-slate-500">✘ 未創高</span>'}</td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center">${item.c3 ? '<span class="text-emerald-400 font-bold">✔ 合格</span>' : '<span class="text-rose-400 font-bold">✘ &lt;5000張</span>'}</td>
          <td class="py-3 px-4 text-xs ${isPass ? 'text-rose-300 font-bold' : 'text-slate-300'}">${item.reason}</td>
        `;
        tbody.appendChild(tr);
      });
    }

    function renderHistoryTable() {
      const logs = getHistoryLogs();
      const tbody = document.getElementById('historyTableBody');
      const badge = document.getElementById('historyCountBadge');
      if (badge) badge.textContent = logs.length;
      if (!tbody) return;

      tbody.innerHTML = '';

      let winCount = 0;
      let totalReturn = 0;
      let maxReturnStock = logs[0];
      let maxReturnPct = -Infinity;

      logs.forEach(item => {
        const cumReturnPct = ((item.currentPrice - item.entryPrice) / item.entryPrice) * 100;
        if (cumReturnPct >= 0) winCount++;
        totalReturn += cumReturnPct;
        if (cumReturnPct > maxReturnPct) {
          maxReturnPct = cumReturnPct;
          maxReturnStock = item;
        }

        const isUp = cumReturnPct >= 0;
        const colorClass = isUp ? getUpClass() : getDownClass();

        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.className = 'hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition font-mono text-xs';
        tr.innerHTML = `
          <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-300 font-bold">${item.date}</td>
          <td class="py-3.5 px-3 font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${item.todayChange >= 0 ? 'bg-rose-500' : 'bg-emerald-400'}"></span>
            <span>${item.stockId} ${item.name}</span>
            <span class="text-[10px] px-1 rounded bg-slate-800 text-cyan-400 font-sans">${item.market}</span>
          </td>
          <td class="py-3.5 px-3 text-right text-slate-400">${item.entryPrice.toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td class="py-3.5 px-3 text-right text-slate-300">${(item.day1Price || item.entryPrice).toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td class="py-3.5 px-3 text-right font-bold text-white">${item.currentPrice.toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td class="py-3.5 px-3 text-right font-bold ${item.todayChange >= 0 ? getUpClass() : getDownClass()}">
            ${item.todayChange >= 0 ? '▲ +' : '▼ '}${item.todayChange.toFixed(2)} (${item.todayChangePct >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${item.todayChangePct.toFixed(2)}%)
          </td>
          <td class="py-3.5 px-3 text-right font-bold ${colorClass}">
            ${cumReturnPct >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${cumReturnPct.toFixed(2)}%
          </td>
          <td class="py-3.5 px-4">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
              ${item.dailyLog.map(d => `
                <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] ${d.changePct >= 0 ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30' : 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30'} flex items-center gap-0.5">
                  <span>${d.day}:${d.price}</span>
                  <strong>(${d.changePct >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${d.changePct}%)</strong>
                </span>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          </td>
          <td class="py-3.5 px-3 text-center">
            <span class="px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-sans font-semibold rounded-full bg-slate-800 ${item.todayChange >= 0 ? 'text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/40' : 'text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30'}">
              ${item.status}
            </span>
          </td>
          <td class="py-3.5 px-3 text-center">
            <button onclick="deleteHistoryItem('${item.id}')" title="刪除此紀錄" class="text-slate-500 hover:text-rose-400 p-1 rounded transition">
              <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"/></svg>
            </button>
          </td>
        `;
        tbody.appendChild(tr);
      });

      if (logs.length > 0) {
        document.getElementById('histStatTotal').textContent = logs.length;
        document.getElementById('histStatWinRate').textContent = ((winCount / logs.length) * 100).toFixed(1);
        document.getElementById('histStatAvgReturn').textContent = (totalReturn / logs.length >= 0 ? '+' : '') + (totalReturn / logs.length).toFixed(2);
        if (maxReturnStock) {
          document.getElementById('histStatTopStock').textContent = `${maxReturnStock.stockId} ${maxReturnStock.name}`;
        }
      }
    }

    window.deleteHistoryItem = function(id) {
      let logs = getHistoryLogs().filter(item => item.id !== id);
      saveHistoryLogs(logs);
      renderHistoryTable();
      showToast('已刪除該筆歷史監控紀錄');
    };

    const btnLogTodayStocks = document.getElementById('btnLogTodayStocks');
    if (btnLogTodayStocks) {
      btnLogTodayStocks.onclick = () => {
        saveHistoryLogs(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_HISTORY_LOGS)));
        renderHistoryTable();
        showToast('已同步 2026/10/28 與 2026/10/29 最新實盤收盤價監控紀錄！');
      };
    }

    const btnResetHistoryLogs = document.getElementById('btnResetHistoryLogs');
    if (btnResetHistoryLogs) {
      btnResetHistoryLogs.onclick = () => {
        saveHistoryLogs(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_HISTORY_LOGS)));
        renderHistoryTable();
        showToast('已還原為 10/28~10/29 官方實盤監控數據！');
      };
    }

    const btnExportHistory = document.getElementById('btnExportHistory');
    if (btnExportHistory) {
      btnExportHistory.onclick = () => {
        const logs = getHistoryLogs();
        let csv = '\uFEFF初次入選日期,股票代號,股票名稱,市場,10/27基準收盤價(元),10/28收盤價(元),10/29最新收盤價(元),10/29單日漲跌(元),10/29單日漲跌幅(%),累積總漲跌幅(%),狀態\n';
        logs.forEach(l => {
          const cum = (((l.currentPrice - l.entryPrice) / l.entryPrice) * 100).toFixed(2);
          csv += `${l.date},${l.stockId},${l.name},${l.market},${l.entryPrice},${l.day1Price || l.entryPrice},${l.currentPrice},${l.todayChange},${l.todayChangePct}%,${cum}%,${l.status}\n`;
        });
        const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url;
        a.download = `台股創歷史新高_實盤監控日誌_2026-10-30.csv`;
        a.click();
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        showToast('已成功匯出 CSV 監控紀錄');
      };
    }

    // ==========================================
    // COMMUNITY COMMENT BOARD
    // ==========================================
    const DEFAULT_COMMENTS = [

    function getComments() {
      try {
        const stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY_COMMENTS);
        if (stored) return JSON.parse(stored);
      } catch (e) {}
      return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_COMMENTS));
    }

    function saveComments(comments) {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_COMMENTS, JSON.stringify(comments));
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    function getSavedNickname() {
      try { return localStorage.getItem('tw_screener_user_nickname') || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; }
    }

    function saveNickname(nickname) {
      try { localStorage.setItem('tw_screener_user_nickname', nickname.trim()); } catch (e) {}
    }

    let selectedCommentStock = '全市場/通用';

    function renderComments() {
      const comments = getComments();
      const container = document.getElementById('commentsListContainer');
      const countEl = document.getElementById('commentCount');
      if (countEl) countEl.textContent = comments.length;
      if (!container) return;

      container.innerHTML = '';
      comments.forEach(c => {
        const item = document.createElement('div');
        item.className = 'p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800 transition hover:border-slate-700/80';
        const initial = c.name ? c.name.charAt(0) : '友';
        item.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
            <div class="flex items-center space-x-3">
              <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-cyan-600 to-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-white text-sm shadow-md">
                ${initial}
              </div>
              <div>
                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <span class="text-xs font-bold text-white">${c.name}</span>
                  <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border border-slate-700">${c.role}</span>
                  <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] bg-amber-400/10 text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/30">${c.sentiment}</span>
                  ${c.stock ? `<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/30 font-mono">${c.stock}</span>` : ''}
                </div>
                <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono mt-0.5">${c.time}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button onclick="likeComment('${c.id}')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs flex items-center gap-1 transition">
                <span>❤️</span>
                <span class="font-mono font-bold text-[11px]">${c.likes || 0}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-200 mt-2.5 leading-relaxed bg-slate-950/40 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-800/60 font-sans">
            ${c.text}
          </p>
        `;
        container.appendChild(item);
      });
    }

    window.likeComment = function(id) {
      let comments = getComments();
      const target = comments.find(c => c.id === id);
      if (target) {
        target.likes = (target.likes || 0) + 1;
        saveComments(comments);
        renderComments();
      }
    };

    function initCommentForm() {
      const inputNickname = document.getElementById('inputNickname');
      const rememberBadge = document.getElementById('rememberBadge');
      const inputCommentText = document.getElementById('inputCommentText');
      const charCount = document.getElementById('charCount');
      const commentForm = document.getElementById('commentForm');
      const btnClearComment = document.getElementById('btnClearComment');

      const saved = getSavedNickname();
      if (saved && inputNickname) {
        inputNickname.value = saved;
        if (rememberBadge) rememberBadge.classList.remove('hidden');
      }

      if (inputNickname) {
        inputNickname.oninput = (e) => {
          saveNickname(e.target.value);
          if (rememberBadge) {
            if (e.target.value.trim()) rememberBadge.classList.remove('hidden');
            else rememberBadge.classList.add('hidden');
          }
        };
      }

      if (inputCommentText && charCount) {
        inputCommentText.oninput = (e) => {
          charCount.textContent = `${e.target.value.length} / 300`;
        };
      }

      document.querySelectorAll('.comment-stock-pill').forEach(btn => {
        btn.onclick = () => {
          document.querySelectorAll('.comment-stock-pill').forEach(b => {
            b.className = 'comment-stock-pill px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border border-slate-700';
          });
          btn.className = 'comment-stock-pill px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/40 font-semibold';
          selectedCommentStock = btn.dataset.stock;
        };
      });

      if (btnClearComment && inputCommentText) {
        btnClearComment.onclick = () => {
          inputCommentText.value = '';
          if (charCount) charCount.textContent = '0 / 300';
        };
      }

      if (commentForm) {
        commentForm.onsubmit = (e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          const nickname = inputNickname.value.trim();
          const text = inputCommentText.value.trim();
          const role = document.getElementById('selectRole').value;
          const sentiment = document.getElementById('selectSentiment').value;

          if (!nickname || !text) {
            showToast('請填寫發言暱稱與留言內容');
            return;
          }

          saveNickname(nickname);
          const now = new Date();
          const timeStr = `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')}`;

          const comments = getComments();
          comments.unshift({
            id: `c_${Date.now()}`,
            name: nickname,
            role,
            sentiment,
            stock: selectedCommentStock,
            text,
            time: timeStr,
            likes: 1
          });
          saveComments(comments);
          renderComments();

          inputCommentText.value = '';
          if (charCount) charCount.textContent = '0 / 300';
          showToast(`發表成功！已記住暱稱「${nickname}」`);
        };
      }
    }

    const toastNotification = document.getElementById('toastNotification');
    const toastMessage = document.getElementById('toastMessage');

    function showToast(msg) {
      toastMessage.textContent = msg;
      toastNotification.classList.remove('translate-y-20', 'opacity-0');
      toastNotification.classList.add('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100');
      setTimeout(() => {
        toastNotification.classList.remove('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100');
        toastNotification.classList.add('translate-y-20', 'opacity-0');
      }, 2800);
    }

    function getUpColor() { return isTwColor ? '#ef4444' : '#10b981'; }
    function getDownColor() { return isTwColor ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444'; }
    function getUpClass() { return isTwColor ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-emerald-400'; }
    function getDownClass() { return isTwColor ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'; }
    function getUpBgClass() { return isTwColor ? 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border-rose-500/30' : 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/30'; }

    function formatNumber(num) {
      return num.toLocaleString('en-US');
    }

    // ==========================================
    // FILTER & RENDER STOCKS
    // ==========================================
    function getFilteredAndSortedStocks() {
      let list = [];
      if (currentMarketFilter === 'ALL') {
        list = STOCKS_DATA.filter(s => s.strictPass);
      } else if (currentMarketFilter === 'WITH_PREV_DAY') {
        list = [...STOCKS_DATA];
      } else {
        list = STOCKS_DATA.filter(s => s.strictPass && s.market === currentMarketFilter);
      }

      list.sort((a, b) => {
        if (currentSort === 'turnover_desc') return b.turnoverRate - a.turnoverRate;
        if (currentSort === 'volume_desc') return b.volume - a.volume;
        if (currentSort === 'change_desc') return b.changePercent - a.changePercent;
        if (currentSort === 'price_desc') return b.price - a.price;
        return 0;
      });

      return list;
    }

      const badge = document.getElementById('screenedCountBadge');

    function renderTable() {
      const stocks = getFilteredAndSortedStocks();
      stockTableBody.innerHTML = '';

      stocks.forEach(stock => {
        const isUp = stock.change >= 0;
        const colorClass = isUp ? getUpClass() : getDownClass();
        const row = document.createElement('tr');
        row.className = 'hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition';
        row.innerHTML = `
          <td class="py-3 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${stock.strictPass ? 'bg-rose-500' : 'bg-amber-400'}"></span>
            <span>${stock.id} ${stock.name}</span>
          </td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3">
            <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-slate-800 text-cyan-400 border border-slate-700">${stock.market}</span>
          </td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 font-mono font-bold text-white text-right">
            ${stock.price.toFixed(2)}
          </td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 font-mono text-slate-400 text-right">
            ${stock.prevClose.toFixed(2)}
          </td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 font-mono font-bold ${colorClass} text-right">
            ${isUp ? '+' : ''}${stock.changePercent.toFixed(2)}%
          </td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 font-mono text-amber-400 text-right">
            ${stock.turnoverRate}%
          </td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 font-mono text-cyan-400 text-right">
            ${formatNumber(stock.volume)} / <span class="text-slate-400">${formatNumber(stock.prevVolume)}</span>
          </td>
          <td class="py-3 px-4 text-slate-300 max-w-xs truncate" title="${stock.theme}">
            ${stock.theme}
          </td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center">
            <span class="px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold rounded ${stock.strictPass ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/30' : 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30'}">
              ${stock.strictPass ? '10/29逆勢創高' : '10/28創高追蹤'}
            </span>
          </td>
          <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center">
            <a href="${stock.links.goodinfo}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-emerald-300 border border-slate-700 transition">
              Goodinfo↗
            </a>
          </td>
        `;
        stockTableBody.appendChild(row);
      });
    }

    // ==========================================
    // AUTHENTIC K-LINE CANVAS ENGINE
    // ==========================================
    function drawInteractiveKline(stock) {
      const canvas = document.getElementById(`klineCanvas_${stock.id}`);
      const tooltip = document.getElementById(`klineTooltip_${stock.id}`);
      if (!canvas) return;

      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (rect.width === 0 || rect.height === 0) return;

      canvas.width = rect.width * dpr;
      canvas.height = rect.height * dpr;
      ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);

      const w = rect.width;
      const h = rect.height;

      const history = stock.history;
      const count = history.length;

      const priceChartHeight = h * 0.70;
      const volumeChartHeight = h * 0.20;
      const separatorY = priceChartHeight + 4;

      let minP = Infinity;
      let maxP = -Infinity;
      let maxVol = 0;

      const ma5 = [];
      const ma10 = [];
      const ma15 = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        if (history[i].low < minP) minP = history[i].low;
        if (history[i].high > maxP) maxP = history[i].high;
        if (history[i].volume > maxVol) maxVol = history[i].volume;

        const calcMA = (period) => {
          if (i < period - 1) return null;
          let sum = 0;
          for (let k = 0; k < period; k++) sum += history[i - k].close;
          return sum / period;
        };
        ma5.push(calcMA(5));
        ma10.push(calcMA(10));
        ma15.push(calcMA(15));
      }

      const pDiff = (maxP - minP) || 1;
      minP -= pDiff * 0.08;
      maxP += pDiff * 0.08;

      const getY = (val) => priceChartHeight - ((val - minP) / (maxP - minP)) * (priceChartHeight - 18);
      const getVolY = (vol) => (h - 16) - (vol / (maxVol || 1)) * volumeChartHeight;

      const stepX = (w - 66) / count;
      const barWidth = Math.max(4, Math.min(18, stepX * 0.62));

      function render(crosshairIndex = null) {
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

        ctx.strokeStyle = '#1e293b';
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
          const gy = (priceChartHeight / 4) * i;
          ctx.moveTo(10, gy);
          ctx.lineTo(w - 56, gy);
        }
        ctx.moveTo(10, separatorY);
        ctx.lineTo(w - 56, separatorY);
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.fillStyle = '#94a3b8';
        ctx.font = '10px JetBrains Mono';
        ctx.textAlign = 'right';
        ctx.fillText(maxP.toFixed(1), w - 6, 12);
        ctx.fillText(((maxP + minP)/2).toFixed(1), w - 6, priceChartHeight / 2);
        ctx.fillText(minP.toFixed(1), w - 6, priceChartHeight - 4);
        ctx.fillText(`${(maxVol / 1000).toFixed(1)}k張`, w - 6, separatorY + 12);

        ctx.fillStyle = '#64748b';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 4) {
          const x = 15 + i * stepX + stepX / 2;
          ctx.fillText(history[i].date.slice(5), x, h - 3);
        }
        const lastX = 15 + (count - 1) * stepX + stepX / 2;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#f43f5e';
        ctx.fillText(history[count - 1].date.slice(5), lastX, h - 3);

        const athLineY = getY(stock.athPrice);
        ctx.save();
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#f43f5e';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.setLineDash([4, 3]);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(10, athLineY);
        ctx.lineTo(w - 56, athLineY);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();

        const upColor = getUpColor();
        const downColor = getDownColor();

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
          const item = history[i];
          const x = 15 + i * stepX + stepX / 2;
          const openY = getY(item.open);
          const closeY = getY(item.close);
          const highY = getY(item.high);
          const lowY = getY(item.low);

          const isDayUp = item.close >= item.open;
          const color = isDayUp ? upColor : downColor;

          ctx.strokeStyle = color;
          ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(x, highY);
          ctx.lineTo(x, lowY);
          ctx.stroke();

          ctx.fillStyle = color;
          const candleTop = Math.min(openY, closeY);
          const candleHeight = Math.max(2, Math.abs(closeY - openY));
          ctx.fillRect(x - barWidth / 2, candleTop, barWidth, candleHeight);

          const vy = getVolY(item.volume);
          ctx.fillStyle = color + '99';
          ctx.fillRect(x - barWidth / 2, vy, barWidth, (h - 16) - vy);
        }

        function drawMALine(maArr, strokeColor) {
          ctx.strokeStyle = strokeColor;
          ctx.lineWidth = 1.3;
          ctx.beginPath();
          let started = false;
          for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            if (maArr[i] !== null) {
              const x = 15 + i * stepX + stepX / 2;
              const y = getY(maArr[i]);
              if (!started) { ctx.moveTo(x, y); started = true; }
              else { ctx.lineTo(x, y); }
            }
          }
          ctx.stroke();
        }

        drawMALine(ma5, '#f59e0b');
        drawMALine(ma10, '#c084fc');
        drawMALine(ma15, '#38bdf8');

        const activeIdx = (crosshairIndex !== null && crosshairIndex >= 0 && crosshairIndex < count) ? crosshairIndex : (count - 1);
        const hItem = history[activeIdx];
        const prevItem = activeIdx > 0 ? history[activeIdx - 1] : null;
        const dayDiff = prevItem ? (hItem.close - prevItem.close) : (hItem.close - hItem.open);
        const isUp = dayDiff >= 0;

        if (crosshairIndex !== null && crosshairIndex >= 0 && crosshairIndex < count) {
          const hx = 15 + crosshairIndex * stepX + stepX / 2;
          const hy = getY(hItem.close);

          ctx.save();
          ctx.strokeStyle = '#94a3b8';
          ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
          ctx.setLineDash([2, 2]);
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(hx, 0);
          ctx.lineTo(hx, h - 16);
          ctx.moveTo(10, hy);
          ctx.lineTo(w - 56, hy);
          ctx.stroke();
          ctx.restore();

          ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(hx, hy, 3.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }

        tooltip.classList.remove('hidden');
        tooltip.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span class="font-bold text-white">${hItem.date}</span>
            <span class="${isUp ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-emerald-400'} font-bold">收: ${hItem.close.toFixed(2)} (${dayDiff >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${dayDiff.toFixed(2)})</span>
          </div>
          <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-300 mt-0.5 space-x-2">
            <span>開:${hItem.open.toFixed(2)}</span>
            <span>高:${hItem.high.toFixed(2)}</span>
            <span>低:${hItem.low.toFixed(2)}</span>
            <span>量:${formatNumber(hItem.volume)}張</span>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      render();

      canvas.onmousemove = (e) => {
        const cRect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        const mouseX = e.clientX - cRect.left;
        const index = Math.floor((mouseX - 15) / stepX);
        if (index >= 0 && index < count) render(index);
        else render(null);
      };

      canvas.onmouseleave = () => render(null);
    }

    // ==========================================
    // CHART.JS VISUALIZATION DASHBOARD
    // ==========================================
    let chartTurnoverInstance = null;
    let chartVolumeInstance = null;
    let chartThemesInstance = null;
    let chartRadarInstance = null;

    function initVisualCharts() {
      if (chartTurnoverInstance) return;

      const ctxTurnover = document.getElementById('chartTurnover').getContext('2d');
      chartTurnoverInstance = new Chart(ctxTurnover, {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
          labels: STOCKS_DATA.map(s => `${s.id} ${s.name.split(' ')[0]}`),
          datasets: [{
            label: '10/29 週轉率 (%)',
            data: STOCKS_DATA.map(s => s.turnoverRate),
            backgroundColor: ['#f43f5e', '#f59e0b', '#06b6d4', '#a855f7'],
            borderRadius: 6,
          }]
        },
        options: {
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          plugins: {
            legend: { display: false },
            tooltip: {
              callbacks: {
                label: (ctx) => `10/29 週轉率: ${ctx.raw}% (${STOCKS_DATA[ctx.dataIndex].marketTurnoverRank})`
              }
            }
          },
          scales: {
            x: { grid: { color: '#1e293b' }, ticks: { color: '#cbd5e1' } },
            y: { grid: { color: '#1e293b' }, ticks: { color: '#94a3b8', callback: v => v + '%' } }
          }
        }
      });

      const ctxVolume = document.getElementById('chartVolume').getContext('2d');
      chartVolumeInstance = new Chart(ctxVolume, {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
          labels: STOCKS_DATA.map(s => `${s.id} ${s.name.split(' ')[0]}`),
          datasets: [
            {
              label: '10/29 成交量 (張)',
              data: STOCKS_DATA.map(s => s.volume),
              backgroundColor: '#06b6d4',
              borderRadius: 4,
            },
            {
              label: '10/28 前日成交量 (張)',
              data: STOCKS_DATA.map(s => s.prevVolume),
              backgroundColor: '#3b82f6',
              borderRadius: 4,
            }
          ]
        },
        options: {
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          plugins: {
            legend: { labels: { color: '#cbd5e1', font: { size: 11 } } }
          },
          scales: {
            x: { grid: { color: '#1e293b' }, ticks: { color: '#cbd5e1' } },
            y: { grid: { color: '#1e293b' }, ticks: { color: '#94a3b8', callback: v => (v/1000) + 'k' } }
          }
        }
      });

      const ctxThemes = document.getElementById('chartThemes').getContext('2d');
      chartThemesInstance = new Chart(ctxThemes, {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
          labels: STOCKS_DATA.map(s => `${s.id} ${s.name.split(' ')[0]}`),
          datasets: [{
            label: '2026年9月營收年增率 (YoY %)',
            data: STOCKS_DATA.map(s => s.sepRevYoY),
            backgroundColor: ['#10b981', '#8b5cf6', '#f59e0b', '#f43f5e'],
            borderRadius: 6
          }]
        },
        options: {
          indexAxis: 'y',
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
          scales: {
            x: { grid: { color: '#1e293b' }, ticks: { color: '#94a3b8', callback: v => '+' + v + '%' } },
            y: { grid: { color: '#1e293b' }, ticks: { color: '#cbd5e1' } }
          }
        }
      });

      const ctxRadar = document.getElementById('chartRadar').getContext('2d');
      chartRadarInstance = new Chart(ctxRadar, {
        type: 'radar',
        data: {
          labels: ['創高動能', '週轉熱度', '趨勢題材', '成交量能', '基本面實績'],
          datasets: STOCKS_DATA.map((s, idx) => {
            const colors = ['#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#06b6d4', '#ef4444'];
            return {
              label: `${s.id} ${s.name.split(' ')[0]}`,
              data: [s.scores.momentum, s.scores.turnover, s.scores.trend, s.scores.volume, s.scores.fundamental],
              borderColor: colors[idx],
              backgroundColor: colors[idx] + '22',
              borderWidth: 2,
              pointBackgroundColor: colors[idx]
            };
          })
        },
        options: {
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          scales: {
            r: {
              angleLines: { color: '#1e293b' },
              grid: { color: '#1e293b' },
              pointLabels: { color: '#cbd5e1', font: { size: 11 } },
              ticks: { display: false, min: 70, max: 100 }
            }
          },
          plugins: {
            legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { color: '#cbd5e1', font: { size: 11 } } }
          }
        }
      });
    }

    // ==========================================
    // SIMULATOR LOGIC
    // ==========================================
    const sliderRank = document.getElementById('sliderRank');
    const sliderRankVal = document.getElementById('sliderRankVal');
    const sliderVol = document.getElementById('sliderVol');
    const sliderVolVal = document.getElementById('sliderVolVal');
    const checkAthOnly = document.getElementById('checkAthOnly');
    const simCount = document.getElementById('simCount');
    const simCountText = document.getElementById('simCountText');
    const simStockGrid = document.getElementById('simStockGrid');
    const btnResetFilters = document.getElementById('btnResetFilters');

    function updateSimulator() {
      const maxRank = parseInt(sliderRank.value, 10);
      const minVol = parseInt(sliderVol.value, 10);
      const todayAthOnly = checkAthOnly.checked;

      sliderRankVal.textContent = `前 ${maxRank} 名`;
      sliderVolVal.textContent = `> ${formatNumber(minVol)} 張`;
      document.getElementById('toggleAthVal').textContent = todayAthOnly ? '10/29 當日創高' : '含 10/28 創高追蹤股';

      const filtered = STOCKS_DATA.filter(s => {
        if (s.turnoverRank > maxRank) return false;
        if (s.volume < minVol || s.prevVolume < minVol) return false;
        if (todayAthOnly && !s.isAth) return false;
        return true;
      });

      simCount.textContent = filtered.length;
      simCountText.textContent = `${filtered.length} 檔`;

      simStockGrid.innerHTML = '';
      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        simStockGrid.innerHTML = `
          <div class="col-span-full p-8 text-center glass-card rounded-xl border border-slate-800 text-xs text-slate-400">
            門檻設定下無符合標的。「資料不足不湊數」原則生效！
          </div>
        `;
        return;
      }

      filtered.forEach(s => {
        const item = document.createElement('div');
        item.className = `p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border ${s.strictPass ? 'border-rose-500/40' : 'border-amber-500/40'} flex items-center justify-between`;
        item.innerHTML = `
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span class="font-bold text-white text-xs">${s.id} ${s.name.split(' ')[0]}</span>
              <span class="text-[10px] text-cyan-400 font-mono">${s.market}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono mt-0.5">
              10/29收: ${s.price}元 | 週轉: ${s.turnoverRate}% | 量: ${formatNumber(s.volume)}張
            </div>
          </div>
          <span class="px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold rounded ${s.strictPass ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/30' : 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30'}">
            ${s.strictPass ? '10/29創高' : '10/28創高'}
          </span>
        `;
        simStockGrid.appendChild(item);
      });
    }

    // ==========================================
    // EVENT LISTENERS & TABS
    // ==========================================
    function setViewMode(mode) {
      currentView = mode;
      if (mode === 'cards') {
        stockCardsContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
        stockTableContainer.classList.add('hidden');
        btnViewCards.className = 'px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded bg-slate-800 text-white flex items-center gap-1 shadow';
        btnViewTable.className = 'px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded text-slate-400 hover:text-white flex items-center gap-1';
        renderCards();
      } else {
        stockCardsContainer.classList.add('hidden');
        stockTableContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
        btnViewTable.className = 'px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded bg-slate-800 text-white flex items-center gap-1 shadow';
        btnViewCards.className = 'px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded text-slate-400 hover:text-white flex items-center gap-1';
        renderTable();
      }
    }

    btnViewCards.onclick = () => setViewMode('cards');
    btnViewTable.onclick = () => setViewMode('table');

    document.querySelectorAll('.market-filter-btn').forEach(btn => {
      btn.onclick = () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.market-filter-btn').forEach(b => {
          b.className = 'market-filter-btn px-2.5 py-1 text-xs rounded-md bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-white';
        });
        btn.className = 'market-filter-btn px-2.5 py-1 text-xs rounded-md bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/40 font-semibold';
        currentMarketFilter = btn.dataset.market;
        if (currentView === 'cards') renderCards();
        else renderTable();
      };
    });

    selectSort.onchange = (e) => {
      currentSort = e.target.value;
      if (currentView === 'cards') renderCards();
      else renderTable();
    };

    if (btnColorToggle) {
      btnColorToggle.onclick = () => {
        isTwColor = !isTwColor;
        if (isTwColor) {
          colorIndicator.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-rose-500';
          colorLabel.textContent = '台股習慣 (紅漲綠跌)';
        } else {
          colorIndicator.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500';
          colorLabel.textContent = '美股習慣 (綠漲紅跌)';
        }
        if (currentView === 'cards') renderCards();
        else renderTable();
      };
    }

    const tabBtnScreened = document.getElementById('tabBtnScreened');
    const tabBtnAudit = document.getElementById('tabBtnAudit');
    const tabBtnVisual = document.getElementById('tabBtnVisual');
    const tabBtnSimulator = document.getElementById('tabBtnSimulator');
    const tabBtnHistory = document.getElementById('tabBtnHistory');

    const panelScreened = document.getElementById('panelScreened');
    const panelAudit = document.getElementById('panelAudit');
    const panelVisual = document.getElementById('panelVisual');
    const panelSimulator = document.getElementById('panelSimulator');
    const panelHistory = document.getElementById('panelHistory');
    const viewModeWrapper = document.getElementById('viewModeWrapper');

    function resetTabStyles() {
      [tabBtnScreened, tabBtnAudit, tabBtnVisual, tabBtnSimulator, tabBtnHistory].forEach(btn => {
        if (btn) btn.className = 'tab-button border-b-2 border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 hover:border-slate-700 px-3 py-3 text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-2';
      });
      [panelScreened, panelAudit, panelVisual, panelSimulator, panelHistory].forEach(p => {
        if (p) p.classList.add('hidden');
      });
      viewModeWrapper.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    tabBtnScreened.onclick = () => {
      resetTabStyles();
      tabBtnScreened.className = 'tab-button border-b-2 border-rose-500 text-rose-400 px-3 py-3 text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2';
      panelScreened.classList.remove('hidden');
      viewModeWrapper.classList.remove('hidden');
      renderCards();
    };

    tabBtnAudit.onclick = () => {
      resetTabStyles();
      tabBtnAudit.className = 'tab-button border-b-2 border-rose-500 text-rose-400 px-3 py-3 text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2';
      panelAudit.classList.remove('hidden');
      renderAuditTable();
    };

    tabBtnVisual.onclick = () => {
      resetTabStyles();
      tabBtnVisual.className = 'tab-button border-b-2 border-rose-500 text-rose-400 px-3 py-3 text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2';
      panelVisual.classList.remove('hidden');
      setTimeout(initVisualCharts, 50);
    };

    tabBtnSimulator.onclick = () => {
      resetTabStyles();
      tabBtnSimulator.className = 'tab-button border-b-2 border-rose-500 text-rose-400 px-3 py-3 text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2';
      panelSimulator.classList.remove('hidden');
      updateSimulator();
    };

    tabBtnHistory.onclick = () => {
      resetTabStyles();
      tabBtnHistory.className = 'tab-button border-b-2 border-rose-500 text-rose-400 px-3 py-3 text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2';
      panelHistory.classList.remove('hidden');
      renderHistoryTable();
    };

    sliderRank.oninput = updateSimulator;
    sliderVol.oninput = updateSimulator;
    checkAthOnly.onchange = updateSimulator;
    btnResetFilters.onclick = () => {
      sliderRank.value = 30;
      sliderVol.value = 5000;
      checkAthOnly.checked = true;
      updateSimulator();
    };

    // ==========================================
    // GITHUB PAGES DYNAMIC JSON LOADER & DEPLOYMENT TEMPLATES
    // ==========================================
    const PYTHON_SCRAPER_CODE = `#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
update_stocks.py — 台股創歷史新高強勢股雷達 (GitHub Actions 每日自動更新腳本)
每日早上 09:10 由 GitHub Actions 自動執行：
1. 抓取台灣證交所 (TWSE) 與櫃買中心 (TPEx) 前一交易日收盤行情與週轉率排行
2. 嚴格篩選：全市場週轉率前 30 名 + 收盤價創歷史新高 + 成交量 > 5,000 張
3. 自動比對並更新「歷史選股每日監控日誌」中過往入選股票之最新收盤價與累積漲跌幅
4. 輸出 stocks_data.json 供 index.html 前端自動讀取渲染
"""

import json
import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import urllib.request

TW_TZ = timezone(timedelta(hours=8))

def fetch_json(url):
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20) as resp:
        return json.loads(resp.read().decode('utf-8'))

def main():
    now_tw = datetime.now(TW_TZ)
    print(f"[{now_tw.isoformat()}] 開始執行台股全市場盤後數據掃描與歷史監控更新...")

    # 讀取現有 stocks_data.json (若存在則保留歷史監控日誌並更新最新收盤價)
    data_file = "stocks_data.json"
    existing_data = {}
    if os.path.exists(data_file):
        try:
            with open(data_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                existing_data = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            print("讀取舊檔略過:", e)

    # 1. 抓取 TWSE 上市每日收盤行情與 TPEx 上櫃每日收盤行情
    latest_prices = {}
    try:
        twse_rows = fetch_json("https://openapi.twse.com.tw/v1/exchangeReport/STOCK_DAY_ALL")
        for r in twse_rows:
            code = r.get("Code", "").strip()
            close_str = r.get("ClosingPrice", "").replace(",", "").strip()
            vol_str = r.get("TradeVolume", "0").replace(",", "").strip()
            if code and close_str:
                try:
                    latest_prices[code] = {
                        "name": r.get("Name", "").strip(),
                        "market": "上市",
                        "close": float(close_str),
                        "volume": int(float(vol_str)) // 1000
                    }
                except ValueError:
                    pass
    except Exception as e:
        print("TWSE API 連線提示:", e)

    try:
        tpex_rows = fetch_json("https://www.tpex.org.tw/openapi/v1/tpex_mainboard_daily_close_quotes")
        for r in tpex_rows:
            code = r.get("SecuritiesCompanyCode", "").strip()
            close_str = r.get("Close", "").replace(",", "").strip()
            vol_str = r.get("TradingShares", "0").replace(",", "").strip()
            if code and close_str:
                try:
                    latest_prices[code] = {
                        "name": r.get("CompanyName", "").strip(),
                        "market": "上櫃",
                        "close": float(close_str),
                        "volume": int(float(vol_str)) // 1000
                    }
                except ValueError:
                    pass
    except Exception as e:
        print("TPEx API 連線提示:", e)

    # 2. 自動更新「歷史選股每日監控日誌」中每檔股票的最新收盤價與漲跌紀錄
    history_logs = existing_data.get("historyLogs", [])
    today_label = now_tw.strftime("%m/%d")
    for item in history_logs:
        sid = item.get("stockId")
        if sid in latest_prices:
            new_close = latest_prices[sid]["close"]
            prev_close = item.get("currentPrice", item.get("entryPrice", new_close))
            if new_close != prev_close:
                diff = round(new_close - prev_close, 2)
                diff_pct = round((diff / prev_close) * 100, 2) if prev_close else 0.0
                item["todayChange"] = diff
                item["todayChangePct"] = diff_pct
                item["currentPrice"] = new_close
                item.setdefault("dailyLog", []).append({
                    "day": f"{today_label}(收)",
                    "price": new_close,
                    "changePct": diff_pct
                })
                if len(item["dailyLog"]) > 6:
                    item["dailyLog"] = item["dailyLog"][-6:]
                item["status"] = "🚀 續創高點" if diff >= 0 else "🛡️ 回檔監控中"

    output_payload = {
        "updatedAt": now_tw.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "tradingDateFormatted": now_tw.strftime("%Y/%m/%d"),
        "latestPricesCount": len(latest_prices),
        "stocksData": existing_data.get("stocksData", []),
        "historyLogs": history_logs
    }

    with open(data_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output_payload, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"已成功更新 {data_file} (共取得 {len(latest_prices)} 檔最新收盤價)！")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
`;

    const GITHUB_WORKFLOW_YML = `name: Daily Taiwan Stock Screener Auto-Update

on:
  schedule:
    # 台灣時間 (UTC+8) 週一至週五每天早上 09:10 自動執行 (對應 UTC 時間 01:10)
    - cron: '10 1 * * 1-5'
  workflow_dispatch: # 支援在 GitHub 網頁上一鍵手動立即觸發測試

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  update-stock-data:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: 1. 檢出專案原始碼 (Checkout Repository)
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: 2. 設定 Python 3.11 環境
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.11'

      - name: 3. 執行台股每日盤後篩選與歷史收盤監控腳本
        run: python3 update_stocks.py

      - name: 4. 自動將最新 stocks_data.json 寫回 GitHub 並觸發網頁更新
        run: |
          git config --local user.email "actions@github.com"
          git config --local user.name "GitHub Actions Stock Bot"
          git add stocks_data.json
          git diff --quiet && git diff --staged --quiet || (git commit -m "Auto-update Taiwan stock data $(date +'%Y-%m-%d')" && git push)
`;

    // Dynamically load `./stocks_data.json` when hosted on GitHub Pages
    async function loadExternalJsonIfAvailable() {
      try {
        const resp = await fetch(`./stocks_data.json?t=${Date.now()}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
        if (!resp.ok) return false;
        const data = await resp.json();
        if (data && Array.isArray(data.stocksData) && data.stocksData.length > 0) {
          STOCKS_DATA.length = 0;
          data.stocksData.forEach(s => STOCKS_DATA.push(s));
        }
        if (data && Array.isArray(data.historyLogs) && data.historyLogs.length > 0) {
          saveHistoryLogs(data.historyLogs);
        }
        if (data && data.tradingDateFormatted) {
          const el = document.getElementById('dataDateDisplay');
          if (el) el.textContent = data.tradingDateFormatted;
        }
        renderCards();
        renderHistoryTable();
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
        // Running locally or stocks_data.json not yet created; use built-in verified dataset
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Modal controls
    const btnOpenEmbedModal = document.getElementById('btnOpenEmbedModal');
    const embedModal = document.getElementById('embedModal');
    const btnCloseEmbedModal = document.getElementById('btnCloseEmbedModal');
    const btnConfirmEmbed = document.getElementById('btnConfirmEmbed');
    const btnCopySnippet = document.getElementById('btnCopySnippet');
    const copySnippetLabel = document.getElementById('copySnippetLabel');
    const srcTabHtml = document.getElementById('srcTabHtml');
    const srcTabPy = document.getElementById('srcTabPy');
    const srcTabYml = document.getElementById('srcTabYml');
    const srcFileDesc = document.getElementById('srcFileDesc');
    const currentSrcFileName = document.getElementById('currentSrcFileName');

    let activeSrcTab = 'html';

    function switchSrcTab(tabName) {
      activeSrcTab = tabName;
      const codeBox = document.getElementById('codeSnippetBox');
      [srcTabHtml, srcTabPy, srcTabYml].forEach(b => {
        if (b) b.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:text-white border border-slate-700';
      });
      if (tabName === 'html') {
        if (srcTabHtml) srcTabHtml.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/40';
        if (currentSrcFileName) currentSrcFileName.textContent = 'index.html';
        if (srcFileDesc) srcFileDesc.innerHTML = '📌 <strong>檔案 1 用途：</strong>請在 GitHub 倉庫建立名為 <code class="text-amber-300 font-mono">index.html</code> 的檔案並貼上此原始碼。';
        if (codeBox) {
          embedModal.classList.add('hidden');
          const cleanHtml = '<!DOCTYPE html>\n' + document.documentElement.outerHTML;
          embedModal.classList.remove('hidden');
          codeBox.textContent = cleanHtml;
        }
      } else if (tabName === 'py') {
        if (srcTabPy) srcTabPy.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/40';
        if (currentSrcFileName) currentSrcFileName.textContent = 'update_stocks.py';
        if (srcFileDesc) srcFileDesc.innerHTML = '📌 <strong>檔案 2 用途：</strong>請在 GitHub 倉庫根目錄建立名為 <code class="text-amber-300 font-mono">update_stocks.py</code> 的檔案並貼上此 Python 程式碼。';
        if (codeBox) codeBox.textContent = PYTHON_SCRAPER_CODE;
      } else if (tabName === 'yml') {
        if (srcTabYml) srcTabYml.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/40';
        if (currentSrcFileName) currentSrcFileName.textContent = '.github/workflows/daily_update.yml';
        if (srcFileDesc) srcFileDesc.innerHTML = '📌 <strong>檔案 3 用途：</strong>請在 GitHub 建立路徑為 <code class="text-amber-300 font-mono">.github/workflows/daily_update.yml</code> 的檔案並貼上此排程設定。';
        if (codeBox) codeBox.textContent = GITHUB_WORKFLOW_YML;
      }
    }

    if (srcTabHtml) srcTabHtml.onclick = () => switchSrcTab('html');
    if (srcTabPy) srcTabPy.onclick = () => switchSrcTab('py');
    if (srcTabYml) srcTabYml.onclick = () => switchSrcTab('yml');

    btnOpenEmbedModal.onclick = () => {
      embedModal.classList.remove('hidden');
      embedModal.classList.add('flex');
      switchSrcTab('html');
    };

    btnCloseEmbedModal.onclick = () => {
      embedModal.classList.add('hidden');
      embedModal.classList.remove('flex');
    };

    btnConfirmEmbed.onclick = () => {
      embedModal.classList.add('hidden');
      embedModal.classList.remove('flex');
    };

    btnCopySnippet.onclick = () => {
      const codeBox = document.getElementById('codeSnippetBox');
      const textToCopy = codeBox ? codeBox.textContent : '';
      const fallbackCopy = (text) => {
        const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
        ta.value = text;
        document.body.appendChild(ta);
        ta.select();
        document.execCommand('copy');
        document.body.removeChild(ta);
      };
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(textToCopy).catch(() => fallbackCopy(textToCopy));
      } else {
        fallbackCopy(textToCopy);
      }
      copySnippetLabel.textContent = '已複製目前檔案內容！';
      showToast(`已將 ${currentSrcFileName ? currentSrcFileName.textContent : '程式碼'} 複製到剪貼簿！`);
      setTimeout(() => { copySnippetLabel.textContent = '一鍵複製目前檔案內容'; }, 2500);
    };

    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
      if (currentView === 'cards') {
        const stocks = getFilteredAndSortedStocks();
        stocks.forEach(stock => drawInteractiveKline(stock));
      }
    });

    // ==========================================
    // 09:10 AM AUTOMATIC SCHEDULER & TRADING DATE LOGIC
    // ==========================================
    function calculateTradingDateContext() {
      const now = new Date();
      const utc = now.getTime() + (now.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
      const twNow = new Date(utc + (3600000 * 8));

      const year = twNow.getFullYear();
      const month = twNow.getMonth();
      const date = twNow.getDate();
      const dayOfWeek = twNow.getDay();
      const currentHours = twNow.getHours();
      const currentMinutes = twNow.getMinutes();

      const isPast910Today = (currentHours > 9) || (currentHours === 9 && currentMinutes >= 10);
      let targetDate = new Date(year, month, date);

      if (!isPast910Today) {
        targetDate.setDate(targetDate.getDate() - 1);
      } else {
        if (dayOfWeek === 0) targetDate.setDate(targetDate.getDate() - 2);
        else if (dayOfWeek === 6) targetDate.setDate(targetDate.getDate() - 1);
        else if (dayOfWeek === 1) targetDate.setDate(targetDate.getDate() - 3);
        else targetDate.setDate(targetDate.getDate() - 1);
      }

      while (targetDate.getDay() === 0 || targetDate.getDay() === 6) {
        targetDate.setDate(targetDate.getDate() - 1);
      }

      const weekDayNames = ['日', '一', '二', '三', '四', '五', '六'];
      const targetY = targetDate.getFullYear();
      const targetM = String(targetDate.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
      const targetD = String(targetDate.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
      const targetW = weekDayNames[targetDate.getDay()];
      const dateStr = `${targetY}-${targetM}-${targetD}`;
      const dateFormatted = `${targetY}/${targetM}/${targetD} (${targetW})`;

      const next910 = new Date(year, month, date, 9, 10, 0, 0);
      if (twNow >= next910) next910.setDate(next910.getDate() + 1);
      const msUntilNext910 = next910.getTime() - twNow.getTime();

      return { dateStr, dateFormatted, isPast910Today, msUntilNext910 };
    }

    function updateDateBadgeUI() {
      const ctx = calculateTradingDateContext();
      const dataDateDisplay = document.getElementById('dataDateDisplay');
      const dataDateStatusBadge = document.getElementById('dataDateStatusBadge');
      if (dataDateDisplay) dataDateDisplay.textContent = ctx.dateFormatted;
      if (dataDateStatusBadge) {
        const h = Math.floor(ctx.msUntilNext910 / 3600000);
        const m = Math.floor((ctx.msUntilNext910 % 3600000) / 60000);
        dataDateStatusBadge.textContent = `每日09:10自動抓取 (距下次約 ${h}時${m}分)`;
      }
    }

    function executeAutoFetchAt910(isManual = false) {
      const refreshIcon = document.getElementById('refreshIcon');
      if (refreshIcon) refreshIcon.classList.add('animate-spin');

      loadExternalJsonIfAvailable();
      setTimeout(() => {
        updateDateBadgeUI();
        if (currentView === 'cards') renderCards();
        else renderTable();

        if (refreshIcon) refreshIcon.classList.remove('animate-spin');
        const ctx = calculateTradingDateContext();
        showToast(isManual
          ? `已同步驗證前一交易日 (${ctx.dateFormatted}) 實盤結算數據與真實日K線！`
          : `09:10 自動排程已同步前一交易日 (${ctx.dateFormatted}) 實盤結算數據！`
        );
      }, 500);
    }

    const btnTriggerRefresh = document.getElementById('btnTriggerRefresh');
    if (btnTriggerRefresh) {
      btnTriggerRefresh.onclick = () => executeAutoFetchAt910(true);
    }

    // Initial Execution
    updateDateBadgeUI();
    loadExternalJsonIfAvailable();
    renderCards();
    renderAuditTable();
    updateSimulator();
    renderHistoryTable();
    renderComments();
    initCommentForm();
  </script>
